Symbol......: c:@F@dot
Module......: linalg
Base name...: dot
Mode........: C
Type........: void (signed long int *, signed long int *, signed long int *, signed long int, signed long int, signed long int, signed long int)
Value.......: 
{
signed long int max_val=bits >= 64 ? 9223372036854775807 : (1 << bits - 1) - 1;

signed long int min_val=bits >= 64 ? -9223372036854775807 - 1 : -(1 << bits - 1);

if(m == 1 && n == 1 && p == 1)
  
{
    signed long int result=*A * *B;

  assert(result >= min_val && result <= max_val, &"integer overflow in dot product"[0]);

  *C = result;

  return;

  }

signed long int i=0;

while(i < m)
  
{
    signed long int j=0;

  while(j < p)
    
{
        signed long int sum=0;

        signed long int k=0;

    while(k < n)
      
{
      sum += A[i * n + k] * B[k * p + j];

      k++;

      }

    assert(sum >= min_val && sum <= max_val, &"integer overflow in dot product"[0]);

    C[i * p + j] = sum;

    j++;

    }

  i++;

  }

}

Symbol......: c:@F@__ESBMC_list_eq
Module......: list
Base name...: __ESBMC_list_eq
Mode........: C
Type........: signed int (const  struct __ESBMC_PyListObj *, const  struct __ESBMC_PyListObj *, unsigned long int, unsigned long int, unsigned long int, unsigned long int)
Value.......: 
{
if(!(_Bool)l1 || !(_Bool)l2)
  return 0;


if(SAME-OBJECT((const void *)l1, (const void *)l2))
  return 1;


if(l1->size != l2->size)
  return 0;


unsigned long int depth_limit=max_depth > 0 ? max_depth : 4;

if(depth_limit > 64)
  depth_limit = 64;


const  struct __ESBMC_PyListObj * [64] stack_a;

const  struct __ESBMC_PyListObj * [64] stack_b;

unsigned long int [64] stack_idx;

signed int top=0;

stack_a[0] = l1;

stack_b[0] = l2;

stack_idx[0] = 0;

top = 1;

while(top > 0)
  
{
    signed int cur=top - 1;

    const  struct __ESBMC_PyListObj * cur_a=stack_a[(signed long int)cur];

    const  struct __ESBMC_PyListObj * cur_b=stack_b[(signed long int)cur];

    unsigned long int idx=stack_idx[(signed long int)cur];

  if(idx >= cur_a->size)
    
{
    top--;

    continue;

    }

  stack_idx[(signed long int)cur] = idx + 1;

    const  struct __ESBMC_PyObj * a=&cur_a->items[(signed long int)idx];

    const  struct __ESBMC_PyObj * b=&cur_b->items[(signed long int)idx];

  if(a->value == b->value)
    continue;


  if(!(_Bool)a->value || !(_Bool)b->value)
    return 0;


  if(a->size != b->size)
    return 0;


  if(a->type_id != b->type_id)
    
{
    if(a->size == 0)
      return 0;


    if(float_type_id != 0 && a->size == 8 && ((signed int)(a->type_id == float_type_id)) != ((signed int)(b->type_id == float_type_id)))
      
{
            double av=a->type_id == float_type_id ? *(const double *)a->value : (double)(*(const signed long int *)a->value);

            double bv=b->type_id == float_type_id ? *(const double *)b->value : (double)(*(const signed long int *)b->value);

      if(av != bv)
        return 0;


      continue;

      }

    if(!(_Bool)(values_equal(a->value, b->value, a->size)))
      return 0;


    continue;

    }

  if(a->type_id == list_type_id)
    
{
        const  struct __ESBMC_PyListObj * nested_a=*(const  struct __ESBMC_PyListObj * *)a->value;

        const  struct __ESBMC_PyListObj * nested_b=*(const  struct __ESBMC_PyListObj * *)b->value;

    if(!(_Bool)nested_a || !(_Bool)nested_b)
      return 0;


    if(SAME-OBJECT((const void *)nested_a, (const void *)nested_b))
      continue;


    if(nested_a->size != nested_b->size)
      return 0;


    if((unsigned long int)top >= depth_limit)
      
{
      assert(0, &"list comparison depth limit exceeded (use --python-list-compare-depth to increase)"[0]);

      return 0;

      }

    stack_a[(signed long int)top] = nested_a;

    stack_b[(signed long int)top] = nested_b;

    stack_idx[(signed long int)top] = 0;

    top++;

    }  else
    
{
        unsigned long int cmp_size=elem_size != 0 ? elem_size : a->size;

    if(!(_Bool)(values_equal(a->value, b->value, cmp_size)))
      return 0;


    }

  }

return 1;

}

Value.......: 
{
assert(l != ( struct __ESBMC_PyListObj *)0 && src != ( struct __ESBMC_PyListObj *)0, &"list_slice_assign: list or source is null"[0]);

assert(step != 0, &"ValueError: slice step cannot be zero"[0]);

signed long int size=(signed long int)l->size;

signed long int lo_clamp=(signed long int)(step < 0 ? -1 : 0);

signed long int hi_clamp=step < 0 ? size - 1 : size;

signed long int start;

if((_Bool)has_lower)
  
{
  start = lower < 0 ? lower + size : lower;

  if(start < lo_clamp)
    start = lo_clamp;


  if(start > hi_clamp)
    start = hi_clamp;


  }else
  start = step < 0 ? size - 1 : 0;


signed long int stop;

if((_Bool)has_upper)
  
{
  stop = upper < 0 ? upper + size : upper;

  if(stop < lo_clamp)
    stop = lo_clamp;


  if(stop > hi_clamp)
    stop = hi_clamp;


  }else
  stop = step < 0 ? (signed long int)(-1) : size;


signed long int slicelen;

if(step < 0)
  slicelen = stop < start ? ((start - stop) - 1) / -step + 1 : 0;
else
  slicelen = start < stop ? ((stop - start) - 1) / step + 1 : 0;


signed long int srclen=(signed long int)src->size;

if(src == l)
  src = list_copy_shallow_sz(l, list_type_id, elem_size);


if(step == 1)
  
{
  if(srclen < slicelen)
    
{
        signed long int to=start + srclen;

        signed long int from=start + slicelen;

    while(from < size)
      l->items[to++] = l->items[from++];


    }  else
    if(srclen > slicelen)
      
{
            signed long int shift=srclen - slicelen;

            signed long int i=size - 1;

      while(i >= start + slicelen)
        
{
        l->items[i + shift] = l->items[i];

        i--;

        }

      }


  l->size = (unsigned long int)((size + srclen) - slicelen);

  }else
  assert(srclen == slicelen, &"ValueError: attempt to assign sequence of different size to extended slice"[0]);


signed long int writelen=srclen;

if(step != 1 && slicelen < writelen)
  writelen = slicelen;


signed long int k=0;

signed long int idx=start;

while(k < writelen)
  
{
  list_store_elem(&l->items[idx], &src->items[k], list_type_id, elem_size);

  idx += step;

  k++;

  }

return 1;

}
